Overall estimate remains approximately 10%; restricted command execution, coding isolation and broader release gates remain incomplete.

Bounded transient file-read retries verified: recognized transient I/O errors receive at most three attempts with 50/100 ms backoff. Each attempt rechecks the lease, reopens and verifies the actual file handle, then reads bounded bytes. Permission denial, missing paths and containment failures do not retry; writes and result persistence are outside the loop. Retry events contain only call ID, attempt and delay. The initial real contention probe `.acceptance/c1a4d7e2-fee2-4dda-99ae-80784f0d46be/` failed because CPython's CRT opener collapsed a Windows sharing violation to EACCES without winerror. Added a Win32 read opener preserving the native error code instead of broadening retries to all access denials. Real source and frozen workers then passed while an exclusive Windows file lock was held until a retry event appeared: `.acceptance/e4346106-4b8f-45e1-9c36-c2a1db2e37c5/` and `.acceptance/892f1b3d-47de-4dcc-816a-de069b59c935/` (build digests). Each recorded one retry/intent, exact content, unchanged bytes/mtime and no unresolved intent. All 153 engine tests, Ruff, strict Pyright, contract checks and native build pass. An existing asynchronous test initially raced verifying-state publication against subsequent lease release; it now waits for both before retaining its lease assertion. Exhausted read intents still require inspection; directory/metadata retries and worker-backed verification/reconciliation remain open. Overall estimate remains approximately 10%; restricted command execution and broader release gates remain incomplete.

Worker-backed discovery/search and specialist reads verified: coordinator directory listing and recursive search now use the owned worker protocol, and specialist read/list calls preserve internal journal results through that same path. Existing outer assignment deadlines cancel/join specialist workers; search retains its cooperative scan budget plus the hard outer worker deadline. All 147 engine tests, Ruff, strict Pyright, generated contracts and frontend/native builds pass. A real worker integration case proves listing/search results and exclusion of internal helper results from parent replay. Real Qwen3.5 9B found a fresh marker and independently read a code absent from the search excerpt: `.acceptance/71796fdf-f885-4edd-a158-2dd7ee9a0a53/`. Rebuilt native plan/researcher/review/dependency continuation passed with actual file evidence, completed fixture reviews, unchanged bytes, restricted PATH and graceful shutdown: `.acceptance/1aef7b98-136f-4a5b-8dc7-3560335e5401/`, including independent `worker-audit.json`. The first native sample `.acceptance/4570e498-cbf3-48cc-9b44-fdc5d6c143a3/` saved IDs inspect_task/review_task instead of fixture-required inspect/review and failed before dispatch; evidence is retained, fixture instructions now explicitly require exact IDs, and graph JSON is saved before validation. Verification, evidence-review and reconciliation filesystem calls still run in process; bounded transient read retries remain unfinished. Overall estimate remains approximately 10%; restricted command/validation execution and broader release gates remain incomplete.

Owned coordinator filesystem workers implemented: direct read/write/create/replace/patch calls now execute through a strict fixed-operation subprocess protocol with bounded private pipes, a ten-second execution deadline clamped to remaining run time, cancellation and process-tree cleanup. Expired budgets/oversized requests are rejected before spawning; worker errors return redacted class names and block the run for inspection. Durable intents and leases remain inside the worker; termination never implies a write did not happen. The frozen engine has an early `--tool-worker` path that avoids API startup/credential lookup. All 146 engine tests, Ruff, strict Pyright, frontend/native builds and contract checks pass. Tests cover real worker journaling/collision feedback, pre-spawn guards, blocked-process timeout/cancellation and coordinator blocked-state handling. Real frozen cancellation observed and stopped the bootloader plus two application descendants while SQLite held the worker, preserving fixture bytes; evidence with executable digests: `.acceptance/72a7faf8-5eea-4b3e-8cce-2757db8af30c/`. Final rebuilt native Qwen3.5 9B read/targeted-edit/exact-check/final-review/shutdown passed: `.acceptance/26ba592d-7bbe-4be8-a1fc-ac39fd17c3bc/`. Earlier new-worker native creation sample `.acceptance/e6eaee14-ffb9-4c1e-8f68-28afee966a6d/` created without the requested newline, corrected it, then blocked on context exhaustion; retained as model reliability evidence. Listing/search, specialists, verification and reconciliation still have in-process filesystem calls; read retries remain open. Rechecked command-backend availability: neither Docker nor Podman installed; OpenSSH exists but no restricted destination is configured. Generated-command execution remains disabled. Details: `docs/architecture/filesystem-workers.md`. Overall estimate remains approximately 10%; Phase 3 and broader release gates remain incomplete.

Native creation and real process-kill recovery verified: rebuilt the desktop/frozen engine and passed the real Qwen3.5 9B create/read/final-review fixture through the native interface. One create proposal matched one create intent, no edit substitute was accepted, exact target/neighbor bytes passed, final acceptance persisted, 29 models were discovered and restricted-PATH startup plus graceful shutdown passed. Evidence: `.acceptance/e29cc00f-d8be-417f-87de-d86e21270ab5/` (`creation.json`, report, executable digests, screenshot and logs). Separate worker-kill tests confirm both creation boundaries: after fsync/before result, recovery confirms applied bytes without rewriting (`.acceptance/5f800d24-7192-4eea-ad53-623788e1d2e9/`); after exclusive creation/before content or execution event, recovery keeps the empty file unresolved/conflicting without rewriting (`.acceptance/ce911f1f-cf19-45f9-aa13-f5cdcaf782f9/`). Both recovered initially to blocked and required explicit reconciliation. Build, Ruff, strict Pyright and contract checks pass; unchanged engine baseline remains 141 tests. Native streaming creation, filesystem-worker timeouts and non-Windows creation support remain open. Restricted commands/project validation still block the Phase 3 gate. Overall release estimate remains approximately 10%.

Exclusive Windows file creation implemented and source-engine live-verified: `workspace_create_file` accepts a plain relative path and at most 64 KiB of UTF-8 content. Handle-relative `NtCreateFile(FILE_CREATE)` never overwrites an existing leaf. Root/parent handles deny rename/delete, every parent is verified and links/junctions are rejected; parent directories must exist. Unsafe aliases/traversal/alternate streams are denied. Durable intent precedes creation; failures after an actual create remain uncertain, including stream setup or fsync failures. Recovery confirms exact destination bytes without rewriting; missing/conflicting destinations remain unresolved. Workspace-mode UI now explains creation permission. All 141 engine tests, Ruff and strict Pyright pass, plus frontend build and seven client tests. Six controlled Edge tests pass (one live browser case skipped). Real Qwen3.5 9B proposed one create, read the fresh random content back, preserved a neighbor and reached verifying with exact bytes. Evidence: `.acceptance/d275bd42-b28b-4621-acbd-493e8a4717bc/report.json` and event database. Official Microsoft API behavior checked; design/limitations in `docs/architecture/file-creation.md`. Native creation rebuild/acceptance, real process-kill creation recovery, worker filesystem timeouts and non-Windows backend remain unverified. Phase 3 still lacks restricted commands/project validation. Overall estimate remains approximately 10%.

Native patch acceptance passed with default non-streaming inference: rebuilt Tauri/frozen engine and used real Qwen3.5 9B through the native interface. Exactly one patch proposal normalized to one journaled write; exact target/neighbor bytes, final fixture review, completed-state persistence, 29-model discovery, restricted PATH and graceful shutdown passed. Screenshot inspected. Evidence: `.acceptance/9fa690db-c717-4255-9de2-d23dc0ee0abc/` including `patch.json`, build digests and logs. Initial streamed attempt `.acceptance/70f352bb-9ea6-4fcc-a1d1-3539d95c167f/` exposed acceptance of an unterminated patch line that removed the file newline; exact verification failed and the correction budget exhausted. Parser now rejects unterminated patch lines unless the conventional no-newline marker explicitly describes file content; regression check added. Rebuilt streamed rerun `.acceptance/a7b85bf5-b7af-47d7-bb60-198bb2512ede/` applied the correct patch/bytes but blocked on an incomplete/invalid final streamed response. Both failures are retained; combined streamed patch/final-review acceptance remains open. All 123 engine tests, Ruff formatting/lint, strict Pyright, seven client tests and generated contract checks pass. Overall estimate remains approximately 10%; creation, restricted commands/validation and the broader release gates remain incomplete.

Strict unified patch tool implemented and live-verified: workspace-mode models can propose a single-file unified diff against an existing UTF-8 file with a required current SHA-256. The parser validates exact matching file headers, ordered non-overlapping hunk coordinates/counts, exact context/line endings, no-final-newline markers, 128-hunk and 64 KiB input/output bounds. It performs no fuzzy application or subprocess execution. Complete output is validated before a normalized hash-checked journaled write; rejected patches save correctable `not_started` feedback. Twelve focused cases include multi-hunk changes, insertion/deletion ranges, CRLF, no final newline, late-hunk rejection with no partial write, malformed patches and size limits. All 123 engine tests, Ruff and strict Pyright pass. Real Qwen3.5 9B called `workspace_read_file` then `workspace_apply_patch`; independent persisted-event inspection confirms one normalized write, exact target newline and unchanged neighbor, ending at verifying. Evidence: `.acceptance/ad4d6aa9-b181-4861-bb1f-17d171e5386a/` (`report.json`, `patch-audit.json`, event database). Native rebuild and patch acceptance remain outstanding. File creation/deletion/rename and multi-file patch transactions are not implemented; restricted commands and validation still block the Phase 3 gate. Overall estimate remains approximately 10%.

Corrective replacement acceptance verified through the complete coordinator: a deterministic provider test covers stale replacement feedback, rereading, a new corrected proposal and persisted conversation replay after reopening storage. The real Qwen3.5 9B diagnostic read the fixture, proposed a deliberately absent match, received one durable `match_count` failure, then submitted a corrected replacement. Exactly one write was recorded; target bytes were exactly `value=2\n`, the neighbor was preserved, no uncertain tool intents remained and the run stopped at verifying. Evidence: `.acceptance/bb4274e6-4760-426c-bd7d-259eb017418f/report.json` and its event database. All 111 engine tests, Ruff and strict Pyright pass. This source-engine sample does not replace native rebuild acceptance or establish model reliability. File creation, apply-patch and restricted command/validation execution remain open. Overall estimate remains approximately 10%.

Targeted replacement precondition recovery: stale hashes, missing/ambiguous matches, invalid UTF-8 and oversized replacement output now persist a failed `workspace.replace_text` proposal with structured `effect: not_started` feedback. The coordinator's existing precondition handling can return that result to the model for correction instead of failing the run. Successful replacements still normalize to the existing journaled write path, preserving write reconciliation. Four new cases prove unchanged bytes, no execution event and no unresolved intent; all 110 engine tests, Ruff lint/format and strict Pyright pass. The first test run exposed incorrect test helper naming and an overlong generated parameter ID; both harness issues were fixed before the passing run. Real-model corrective replacement acceptance and native rebuild remain outstanding. Exclusive file creation, patch application and restricted validation execution remain required Phase 3 work. Overall estimate remains approximately 10%.

Desktop event reconnection implemented: unexpected EOF (including a truncated event), network errors and selected transient HTTP statuses retry up to five times with 250 msÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â‚¬Å“4 s backoff. Each request uses the last delivered sequence as `Last-Event-ID`; duplicate replay events are ignored and incomplete frames are discarded. Explicit stream completion ends normally. Authentication, malformed events and sequence gaps remain visible failures; aborting the view cancels retry backoff. Seven client tests and TypeScript checks pass. Two controlled Edge streaming scenarios pass, including a truncated connection followed by duplicate replay and a saved final candidate, with cursors exactly `0,1` and the partial view cleared. Engine restart changes native connection credentials/address and still requires app reconnection; this does not claim live engine-restart or network-fault native acceptance. Overall estimate remains approximately 10%.

Native streamed edit and final review verified: rebuilt the frozen engine and Tauri application, then ran the real Qwen3.5 9B targeted-edit fixture with `XAA_STREAM_RESPONSES=1`. The native Partial response view was observed; nine public content delta events were persisted. The event audit found three validated tool proposals and confirmed each recorded write execution followed its complete proposal. Streamed coordinator inference: added bounded typed assembly of tool ID/name/argument fragments, finish-marker validation, contiguous tool-index checks, duplicate-ID rejection and JSON-object argument validation. Partial content produces batched durable progress events and a separate Partial response view; it is excluded from conversation replay and cannot become an executable action or reviewable candidate. Hidden reasoning fields are ignored. At this earlier implementation checkpoint, `XAA_STREAM_RESPONSES=1` enabled coordinator streaming, while the desktop default was unchanged and specialist responses were buffered; subsequent sections document the desktop default and task-scoped specialist deltas. Extracted tool dispatch and plan proposal handling after the expanded coordinator exceeded Pyright's control-flow analysis limit. All 106 engine tests, Ruff, strict Pyright, frontend build, four client tests and contract checks pass. Five controlled browser scenarios pass (live browser scenario skipped), including partial text remaining distinct from final candidates. Cancellation closes the stream and releases the shared inference queue; unfinished write arguments or a missing finish marker produce no tool intent or file change. Real Qwen3.5 9B streamed search/read calls and a correct fresh-code answer with five content delta events: `.acceptance/5d49a3c0-1782-4fe2-8f75-6d0a6ef1f144/`. Initial live attempt `.acceptance/04b86273-dc33-44ce-9720-d96e5a8806db/` executed the read tools but returned no public final answer; that failure is retained and empty public responses now reject at assembly. Official LM Studio streaming documentation checked. Native streaming acceptance, per-model streaming diagnostics and reliability benchmarks were incomplete at that checkpoint; overall estimate approximately 10%.

Native history/reload acceptance PASSED: rebuilt the bundled desktop/engine and booted an isolated snapshot of the previously completed real-model edit run. Saved history reopened its accepted result and activity in the fresh native process and again after a WebView reload; all 29 execution events remained byte-for-byte equivalent through the API, proving the read path did not repeat execution.
